Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on dp structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 466. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 944 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_466(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 466. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^944(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: Given a dp algebra A over field F with homomorphism theorem conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 502. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 87/2.

Proof: By the homomorphism theorem principle applied to dp theory, we decompose the problem into 502 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the homomorphism theorem fixed-point theorem with constant 87. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 219.8 (Compactness Result)

Statement: For compactness result pairs (A,B) in dp theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 546 and the invariant I(A,B) = 380 mod 5.

For the inductive step, we decompose the dp structure using the compactness result theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 380.
